{"forms":[{"form":"smiles","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"She's got a perfect smile.","type":"example"},{"text":"He has a sinister smile.","type":"example"},{"text":"She had a smile on her face.","type":"example"},{"text":"He always puts a smile on my face.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A facial expression comprised by flexing the muscles of both ends of one's mouth, often showing the front teeth, without vocalisation, and in humans is a common involuntary or voluntary expression of happiness, pleasure, amusement, goodwill, or anxiety."]},{"examples":[{"text":"the smile of the gods","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Favour; propitious regard."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"glosses":["A drink bought by one person for another."],"synonyms":[{"word":"treat"}],"tags":["dated","slang"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈsmaɪl/"},{"ipa":"/ˈsmaɪ.əl/"}],"synonyms":[{"word":"smile"}],"upstream_line":23064,"upstream_offset":156864892,"upstream_record_sha256":"30424027e7b92b0dc87b0355281f99d962c30da56af97a48a88126a8008df0d7","word":"smile"}
{"forms":[{"form":"smiles","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"smiling","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"smiled","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"smiled","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"When you smile, the whole world smiles with you.","type":"example"},{"text":"I don't know what he's smiling about.","type":"example"},{"text":"She smiles a beautiful smile.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To have (a smile) on one's face."],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to smile consent, or a welcome","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To express by smiling."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To express amusement, pleasure, or love and kindness."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The sun smiled down from a clear summer sky.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To look cheerful and joyous; to have an appearance suited to excite joy."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The gods smiled on his labours.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be propitious or favourable; to countenance."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["Of ackee fruit: to open fully, indicating that it is no longer toxic, and ready to be picked."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈsmaɪl/"},{"ipa":"/ˈsmaɪ.əl/"}],"upstream_line":23065,"upstream_offset":156939919,"upstream_record_sha256":"5a0a16107650547e096d452389af27557070a78fce5b9f60f0f622c555160c4a","word":"smile"}
{"forms":[{"form":"smoke tests","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A test for leaks involving blowing smoke into a tube or pipe."]},{"glosses":["A preliminary test on a newly-constructed piece of electronic equipment, consisting simply of the application of electrical power, to make sure that no egregious wiring errors exist which would cause the circuitry to emit smoke, catch fire, explode, etc."]},{"glosses":["Such an initial test on some other (not necessarily electronic) system, such as a computer program, again, not to perform any exhaustive tests of functionality, but simply to make sure that the system will not catastrophically fail on activation."],"tags":["broadly"]},{"glosses":["A test to make sure that theatrical smoke will not set off the smoke alarms in a building."]}],"upstream_line":556534,"upstream_offset":2542034202,"upstream_record_sha256":"1d0e2b7b1dce37a7da72b4654e93df0c4b8c4381cb3aeb0c807f2d821d89c8ac","word":"smoke test"}
